Fallback: recurse dict values\n        for v in catalog_obj.values():\n            yield from _iter_catalog_therapies(v)\n\n\ndef _get_aliases(therapy_entry):\n    aliases = []\n    for key in (\"aliases\", \"alias\", \"aka\", \"alternate_names\", \"alt_names\"):\n        if key not in therapy_entry:\n            continue\n        v = therapy_entry.get(key)\n        if v is None:\n            continue\n        if isinstance(v, list):\n            aliases.extend([str(x) for x in v if str(x).strip() != \"\"])\n        else:\n            s = str(v).strip()\n            if s:\n                aliases.append(s)\n    return aliases\n\n\ndef main():\n    # Load therapy catalog\n    with THERAPY_CATALOG_PATH.open(\"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        catalog = json.load(f)\n\n    all_therapies = list(_iter_catalog_therapies(catalog))\n    in_scope = [t for t in all_therapies if _is_true(t.get(\"include_in_review\"))]\n\n    # Index in-scope therapies\n    therapies_by_code = {}\n    for t in in_scope:\n        code = str(t.get(\"therapy_code\", \"\")).strip()\n        if not code:\n            continue\n        therapies_by_code[code] = t\n\n    in_scope_codes = set(therapies_by_code.keys())\n\n    # Load bag supply costs\n    bag_rows = _read_csv_dicts(BAG_SUPPLY_COST_PATH)\n    bag_cost_by_size_ml = {}\n    for r in bag_rows:\n        size_ml = _to_int(r.get(\"bag_size_ml\"))\n        cost = _to_decimal(r.get(\"bag_supply_cost_usd\"))\n        bag_cost_by_size_ml[size_ml] = cost\n\n    # Resolve delivery payments by matching therapy_label to therapy name OR alias\n    payment_rows = _read_csv_dicts(DELIVERY_PAYMENT_PATH)\n\n    label_to_code = {}\n    # Deterministic: build labels in therapy_code sort order\n    for code in sorted(in_scope_codes):\n        t = therapies_by_code[code]\n        names = [t.get(\"therapy_name\")]\n        names.extend(_get_aliases(t))\n        for name in names:\n            if name is None:\n                continue\n            norm = _norm_label(name)\n            if not norm:\n                continue\n            # Keep first mapping for determinism if collisions exist\n            label_to_code.setdefault(norm, code)\n\n    payment_by_code = {}\n    for r in payment_rows:\n        label = r.get(\"therapy_label\")\n        norm = _norm_label(label)\n        code = label_to_code.get(norm)\n        if not code:\n            continue\n        payment_by_code[code] = _to_decimal(r.get(\"payment_per_delivery_per_patient_usd\"))\n\n    # Resolve active patients from overrides\n    override_rows = _read_csv_dicts(PATIENT_OVERRIDES_PATH)\n    if override_rows:\n        override_headers = set(override_rows[0].keys())\n    else:\n        override_headers = set()\n\n    patient_field = \"active_patients\" if \"active_patients\" in override_headers else \"patient_count\"\n\n    best_override_by_code = {}\n    for r in override_rows:\n        code = str(r.get(\"therapy_code\", \"\")).strip()\n        if code not in in_scope_codes:\n            continue\n        status = str(r.get(\"status\", \"\")).strip()\n        if status != \"approved\":\n            continue\n        rev = _to_int(r.get(\"revision\"), default=-1)\n        prev = best_override_by_code.get(code)\n        if prev is None or rev > prev[\"revision_int\"]:\n            best_override_by_code[code] = {\n                \"revision_int\": rev,\n                \"row\": r,\n            }\n\n    active_patients_by_code = {}\n    for code, info in best_override_by_code.items():\n        r = info[\"row\"]\n        active_patients_by_code[code] = _to_int(r.get(patient_field), default=0)\n\n    # Compute per-therapy metrics\n    results = []\n    for code in sorted(in_scope_codes):\n        t = therapies_by_code[code]\n        therapy_name = str(t.get(\"therapy_name\", \"\")).strip()\n\n        drug_cost_per_1000 = _to_decimal(t.get(\"drug_cost_per_1000_mg_usd\"))\n        dose_mg_per_day = _to_decimal(t.get(\"dose_mg_per_day\"))\n        bag_size_ml = _to_int(t.get(\"bag_size_ml\"))\n\n        if bag_size_ml not in bag_cost_by_size_ml:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Missing bag supply cost for bag_size_ml={bag_size_ml} (therapy_code={code})\")\n        bag_supply_cost = bag_cost_by_size_ml[bag_size_ml]\n\n        active_patients = active_patients_by_code.get(code, 0)\n        payment_per_delivery = payment_by_code.get(code, Decimal(\"0\"))\n\n        def model_block(days_per_delivery: int, deliveries_per_year: int):\n            annual_drug_cost = (\n                drug_cost_per_1000\n                * Decimal(active_patients)\n                * dose_mg_per_day\n                * Decimal(days_per_delivery)\n                * Decimal(deliveries_per_year)\n                / Decimal(\"1000\")\n            )\n            annual_supply_cost = bag_supply_cost * Decimal(active_patients) * Decimal(deliveries_per_year)\n            annual_revenue = payment_per_delivery * Decimal(active_patients) * Decimal(deliveries_per_year)\n            annual_margin = annual_revenue - annual_drug_cost - annual_supply_cost\n            return {\n                \"days_per_delivery\": days_per_delivery,\n                \"deliveries_per_year\": deliveries_per_year,\n                \"annual_drug_cost_usd\": _money_str(annual_drug_cost),\n                \"annual_supply_cost_usd\": _money_str(annual_supply_cost),\n                \"annual_revenue_usd\": _money_str(annual_revenue),\n                \"annual_margin_usd\": _money_str(annual_margin),\n                # Keep numeric for internal aggregation\n                \"_annual_margin_dec\": _money_decimal(annual_margin),\n            }\n\n        m7 = model_block(7, 52)\n        m14 = model_block(14, 26)\n\n        margin_diff = m14[\"_annual_margin_dec\"] - m7[\"_annual_margin_dec\"]\n\n        results.append(\n            {\n                \"therapy_code\": code,\n                \"therapy_name\": therapy_name,\n                \"active_patients\": active_patients,\n                \"drug_cost_per_1000_mg_usd\": _money_str(drug_cost_per_1000),\n                \"dose_mg_per_day\": float(dose_mg_per_day) if dose_mg_per_day % 1 != 0 else int(dose_mg_per_day),\n                \"bag_size_ml\": bag_size_ml,\n                \"bag_supply_cost_usd\": _money_str(bag_supply_cost),\n                \"payment_per_delivery_per_patient_usd\": _money_str(payment_per_delivery),\n                \"model_7_day\": {\n                    k: v for k, v in m7.items() if not k.startswith(\"_\")\n                },\n                \"model_14_day\": {\n                    k: v for k, v in m14.items() if not k.startswith(\"_\")\n                },\n                \"annual_margin_difference_14_minus_7_usd\": _money_str(margin_diff),\n                \"_annual_margin_7_dec\": m7[\"_annual_margin_dec\"],\n                \"_annual_margin_14_dec\": m14[\"_annual_margin_dec\"],\n                \"_annual_margin_diff_dec\": _money_decimal(margin_diff),\n            }\n        )\n\n    # Totals\n    total_7 = sum((t[\"_annual_margin_7_dec\"] for t in results), Decimal(\"0.00\"))\n    total_14 = sum((t[\"_annual_margin_14_dec\"] for t in results), Decimal(\"0.00\"))\n    total_diff = sum((t[\"_annual_margin_diff_dec\"] for t in results), Decimal(\"0.00\"))\n\n    total_7_q = _money_decimal(total_7)\n    total_14_q = _money_decimal(total_14)\n    total_diff_q = _money_decimal(total_diff)\n    abs_total_diff_q = _money_decimal(abs(total_diff_q))\n\n    threshold = Decimal(\"15000\")\n    if abs_total_diff_q < threshold:\n        decision = \"move_to_14_day\"\n    else:\n        decision = \"keep_7_day\"\n\n    justification = (\n        f\"Absolute total annual margin difference (14 minus 7) is ${_money_str(abs_total_diff_q)} \"\n        f\"against a ${_money_str(threshold)} threshold; total 7-day margin=${_money_str(total_7_q)}, \"\n        f\"total 14-day margin=${_money_str(total_14_q)}.\"\n    )\n\n    # Strip internal fields before writing\n    therapies_out = []\n    for t in results:\n        t2 = dict(t)\n        for k in [\n            \"_annual_margin_7_dec\",\n            \"_annual_margin_14_dec\",\n            \"_annual_margin_diff_dec\",\n        ]:\n            t2.pop(k, None)\n        therapies_out.append(t2)\n\n    output = {\n        \"assumptions\": {\n            \"deliveries_per_year_7_day\": 52,\n            \"deliveries_per_year_14_day\": 26,\n            \"days_per_delivery_7_day\": 7,\n            \"days_per_delivery_14_day\": 14,\n            \"switch_threshold_usd\": 15000,\n            \"patient_override_rule\": \"highest approved revision per therapy_code\",\n        },\n        \"therapies\": therapies_out,\n        \"totals\": {\n            \"total_annual_margin_7_day_usd\": _money_str(total_7_q),\n            \"total_annual_margin_14_day_usd\": _money_str(total_14_q),\n            \"total_annual_margin_difference_14_minus_7_usd\": _money_str(total_diff_q),\n            \"absolute_total_margin_difference_usd\": _money_str(abs_total_diff_q),\n        },\n        \"recommendation\": {\n            \"decision\": decision,\n            \"justification\": justification,\n        },\n    }\n\n    with OUTPUT_ANALYSIS_PATH.open(\"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\n        f.write(\"\\n\")\n\n    # Summary markdown (4-8 non-empty lines)\n    total_7_f = float(total_7_q)\n    total_14_f = float(total_14_q)\n    abs_diff_f = float(abs_total_diff_q)\n\n    lines = [\n        \"Infusion batch margin summary\",\n        f\"Total 7-day margin: ${'{:,.2f}'.format(total_7_f)}\",\n        f\"Total 14-day margin: ${'{:,.2f}'.format(total_14_f)}\",\n        f\"Absolute difference: ${'{:,.2f}'.format(abs_diff_f)}\",\n        f\"Decision: {decision}\",\n    ]\n\n    with OUTPUT_SUMMARY_PATH.open(\"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        f.write(\"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"}}],"model":"openai/gpt-5.2-20251211","stop_reason":null,"stop_details":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":null,"cache_read_input_tokens":null,"cache_creation":null,"inference_geo":null,"server_tool_use":null,"service_tier":null,"speed":"standard"},"provider":"OpenAI","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"78e7c87b-a476-41e2-ac18-082b318607c5","uuid":"8b42a3a1-f2bd-4871-b7cb-d822ccc7750f"}

Index in-scope therapies\n    therapies_by_code = {}\n    for t in in_scope:\n        code = str(t.get(\"therapy_code\", \"\")).strip()\n        if not code:\n            continue\n        therapies_by_code[code] = t\n\n    in_scope_codes = set(therapies_by_code.keys())\n\n    # Load bag supply costs\n    bag_rows = _read_csv_dicts(BAG_SUPPLY_COST_PATH)\n    bag_cost_by_size_ml = {}\n    for r in bag_rows:\n        size_ml = _to_int(r.get(\"bag_size_ml\"))\n        cost = _to_decimal(r.get(\"bag_supply_cost_usd\"))\n        bag_cost_by_size_ml[size_ml] = cost\n\n    # Resolve delivery payments by matching therapy_label to therapy name OR alias\n    payment_rows = _read_csv_dicts(DELIVERY_PAYMENT_PATH)\n\n    label_to_code = {}\n    # Deterministic: build labels in therapy_code sort order\n    for code in sorted(in_scope_codes):\n        t = therapies_by_code[code]\n        names = [t.get(\"therapy_name\")]\n        names.extend(_get_aliases(t))\n        for name in names:\n            if name is None:\n                continue\n            norm = _norm_label(name)\n            if not norm:\n                continue\n            # Keep first mapping for determinism if collisions exist\n            label_to_code.setdefault(norm, code)\n\n    payment_by_code = {}\n    for r in payment_rows:\n        label = r.get(\"therapy_label\")\n        norm = _norm_label(label)\n        code = label_to_code.get(norm)\n        if not code:\n            continue\n        payment_by_code[code] = _to_decimal(r.get(\"payment_per_delivery_per_patient_usd\"))\n\n    # Compute per-therapy metrics\n    results = []\n    for code in sorted(in_scope_codes):\n        t = therapies_by_code[code]\n        therapy_name = str(t.get(\"therapy_name\", \"\")).strip()\n\n        drug_cost_per_1000 = _to_decimal(t.get(\"drug_cost_per_1000_mg_usd\"))\n        dose_mg_per_day = _to_decimal(t.get(\"dose_mg_per_day\"))\n        bag_size_ml = _to_int(t.get(\"bag_size_ml\"))\n\n        if bag_size_ml not in bag_cost_by_size_ml:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Missing bag supply cost for bag_size_ml={bag_size_ml} (therapy_code={code})\")\n        bag_supply_cost = bag_cost_by_size_ml[bag_size_ml]\n\n        active_patients = active_patients_by_code.get(code, 0)\n        payment_per_delivery = payment_by_code.get(code, Decimal(\"0\"))\n\n        def model_block(days_per_delivery: int, deliveries_per_year: int):\n            annual_drug_cost = (\n                drug_cost_per_1000\n                * Decimal(active_patients)\n                * dose_mg_per_day\n                * Decimal(days_per_delivery)\n                * Decimal(deliveries_per_year)\n                / Decimal(\"1000\")\n            )\n            annual_supply_cost = bag_supply_cost * Decimal(active_patients) * Decimal(deliveries_per_year)\n            annual_revenue = payment_per_delivery * Decimal(active_patients) * Decimal(deliveries_per_year)\n            annual_margin = annual_revenue - annual_drug_cost - annual_supply_cost\n            return {\n                \"days_per_delivery\": days_per_delivery,\n                \"deliveries_per_year\": deliveries_per_year,\n                \"annual_drug_cost_usd\": _money_str(annual_drug_cost),\n                \"annual_supply_cost_usd\": _money_str(annual_supply_cost),\n                \"annual_revenue_usd\": _money_str(annual_revenue),\n                \"annual_margin_usd\": _money_str(annual_margin),\n                # Keep numeric for internal aggregation\n                \"_annual_margin_dec\": _money_decimal(annual_margin),\n            }\n\n        m7 = model_block(7, 52)\n        m14 = model_block(14, 26)\n\n        margin_diff = m14[\"_annual_margin_dec\"] - m7[\"_annual_margin_dec\"]\n\n        results.append(\n            {\n                \"therapy_code\": code,\n                \"therapy_name\": therapy_name,\n                \"active_patients\": active_patients,\n                \"drug_cost_per_1000_mg_usd\": _money_str(drug_cost_per_1000),\n                \"dose_mg_per_day\": float(dose_mg_per_day) if dose_mg_per_day % 1 != 0 else int(dose_mg_per_day),\n                \"bag_size_ml\": bag_size_ml,\n                \"bag_supply_cost_usd\": _money_str(bag_supply_cost),\n                \"payment_per_delivery_per_patient_usd\": _money_str(payment_per_delivery),\n                \"model_7_day\": {\n                    k: v for k, v in m7.items() if not k.startswith(\"_\")\n                },\n                \"model_14_day\": {\n                    k: v for k, v in m14.items() if not k.startswith(\"_\")\n                },\n                \"annual_margin_difference_14_minus_7_usd\": _money_str(margin_diff),\n                \"_annual_margin_7_dec\": m7[\"_annual_margin_dec\"],\n                \"_annual_margin_14_dec\": m14[\"_annual_margin_dec\"],\n                \"_annual_margin_diff_dec\": _money_decimal(margin_diff),\n            }\n        )\n\n    # Totals\n    total_7 = sum((t[\"_annual_margin_7_dec\"] for t in results), Decimal(\"0.00\"))\n    total_14 = sum((t[\"_annual_margin_14_dec\"] for t in results), Decimal(\"0.00\"))\n    total_diff = sum((t[\"_annual_margin_diff_dec\"] for t in results), Decimal(\"0.00\"))\n\n    total_7_q = _money_decimal(total_7)\n    total_14_q = _money_decimal(total_14)\n    total_diff_q = _money_decimal(total_diff)\n    abs_total_diff_q = _money_decimal(abs(total_diff_q))\n\n    threshold = Decimal(\"15000\")\n    if abs_total_diff_q < threshold:\n        decision = \"move_to_14_day\"\n    else:\n        decision = \"keep_7_day\"\n\n    justification = (\n        f\"Absolute total annual margin difference (14 minus 7) is ${_money_str(abs_total_diff_q)} \"\n        f\"against a ${_money_str(threshold)} threshold; total 7-day margin=${_money_str(total_7_q)}, \"\n        f\"total 14-day margin=${_money_str(total_14_q)}.\"\n    )\n\n    # Strip internal fields before writing\n    therapies_out = []\n    for t in results:\n        t2 = dict(t)\n        for k in [\n            \"_annual_margin_7_dec\",\n            \"_annual_margin_14_dec\",\n            \"_annual_margin_diff_dec\",\n        ]:\n            t2.pop(k, None)\n        therapies_out.append(t2)\n\n    output = {\n        \"assumptions\": {\n            \"deliveries_per_year_7_day\": 52,\n            \"deliveries_per_year_14_day\": 26,\n            \"days_per_delivery_7_day\": 7,\n            \"days_per_delivery_14_day\": 14,\n            \"switch_threshold_usd\": 15000,\n            \"patient_override_rule\": \"highest approved revision per therapy_code\",\n        },\n        \"therapies\": therapies_out,\n        \"totals\": {\n            \"total_annual_margin_7_day_usd\": _money_str(total_7_q),\n            \"total_annual_margin_14_day_usd\": _money_str(total_14_q),\n            \"total_annual_margin_difference_14_minus_7_usd\": _money_str(total_diff_q),\n            \"absolute_total_margin_difference_usd\": _money_str(abs_total_diff_q),\n        },\n        \"recommendation\": {\n            \"decision\": decision,\n            \"justification\": justification,\n        },\n    }\n\n    with OUTPUT_ANALYSIS_PATH.open(\"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\n        f.write(\"\\n\")\n\n    #
